### [66] [FAVA: Formal Authorization for Verified Agents with Evidence-Backed Permission Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27267)
*Yifan Zhang,Xinkui Zhao,Sai Liu,Hengxuan Lou,Guanjie Cheng,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FAVA（形式化授权框架），通过LLM引导的权限中间表示（IR）将自然语言任务转化为结构化约束，再利用确定性降低流程生成带有证据的权限图，最终由SMT求解器对安全策略进行数学验证，并在运行时网关强制执行授权或拦截结果，从而实现智能体执行过程中的上下文感知安全授权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在动态环境中自主交错执行语义推理与复杂系统操作，静态的工具级权限无法满足安全需求，因为安全授权高度依赖上下文、运行时状态和数据流。需要一个能够随环境动态变化而进行严格验证的授权机制。

Method: FAVA框架包含四个核心组件：(1) LLM引导的权限中间表示（Permission IR），将模糊的自然语言任务翻译为结构化约束；(2) 确定性降低过程，将IR转换为带证据的权限图，显式追踪数据流、依赖关系和上下文标签；(3) SMT授权器，在任何有副作用动作执行前，通过Satisfiability Modulo Theories数学验证当前权限图是否符合安全策略；(4) 运行时网关，强制执行求解器结果，授权执行或通过精确反例进行拦截。

Result: 在OpenAgentSafety、OctoBench和ActPlane三个场景的评估中，FAVA在聚合数据集上实现了90.5%的决策合规率（DCR），成功在trace-conditioned场景中拦截了动态违规执行轨迹。

Conclusion: FAVA证明将形式化验证方法（SMT）与LLM引导的权限建模相结合，能够为智能体提供可验证的、上下文感知的运行时安全授权保障，弥补了静态权限体系的不足。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents autonomously interleave semantic reasoning with complex system operations. In these dynamic environments, static tool-level permissions are fundamentally insufficient; safe authorization is highly context-dependent and heavily reliant on evolving runtime states and data flows. We present FAVA (Formal Authorization for Verified Agents), a permission-carrying authorization framework for agent execution. FAVA utilizes an LLM-guided Permission Intermediate Representation (IR) to translate ambiguous natural-language tasks into structured constraints. A deterministic lowering pass then converts this IR into an evidence-backed permission graph that explicitly tracks data flows, dependencies, and contextual labels. To provide strict security guarantees, a Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT) authorizer mathematically verifies the current graph against security policies before any effectful action executes. A runtime gateway then enforces the solver's result, either authorizing the execution or intercepting it with a precise counterexample. We evaluate FAVA across OpenAgentSafety, OctoBench, and ActPlane scenarios. Our evaluation demonstrates that FAVA achieves a 90.5% Decision Compliance Rate (DCR) over the aggregate dataset, successfully intercepting dynamic violating traces in the evaluated trace-conditioned scenarios.

</details>


### [67] [Open Security Benchmark: Towards Autonomous Enterprise Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27288)
*Gal Engelberg,Michael Arenzon,Leon Goldberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Open Security Benchmark (OSB)框架，用于端到端评估企业自主网络防御中的智能体AI。OSB通过冻结的、跨厂商的企业安全态势环境快照，支持文本转SQL和厂商原生API两种调查模态的基准测试，包含数据层、任务/评测层、多维度评分层、可审计框架和自带路径等五个组件，并配套身份安全包与合成组织数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业正转向自主网络防御，依赖智能体AI构建安全态势感知并据此决策行动。然而当前无法评估这类智能体是否值得信任：真实企业环境是私有、跨厂商且深度关联的，没有共享可查询的公开目标用于端到端评估，这一"环境数据缺口"阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: OSB框架包含五个组件：(1)数据层，提供冻结的、涵盖身份/云/基础设施/数据/应用及配置的跨厂商企业环境快照；(2)任务与评测集层；(3)多维度评分层；(4)最小可审计的执行框架；(5)自带路径（BYO），支持公共对比与私有租户评估。框架提供两种评估模态：对关系型快照的text-to-SQL，以及在同一环境的服务实例上调用各厂商原生API。冻结环境将目标状态锚定为不可变快照，并以封闭形式的地面真值约束答案。

Result: 作者用两个身份安全评测包和一系列覆盖多规模的合成组织环境数据集实例化了该框架，并规划了向更多态势子域、调查模态以及从评估到修复的防御阶段的扩展路径。

Conclusion: OSB填补了企业自主网络防御中智能体评估的环境数据缺口，为可信智能体AI在安全态势管理中的验证提供了统一基准平台。

Abstract: Enterprises are moving toward autonomous cyber defense: agentic AI that builds situational awareness of an organization's security state and reasons from it to assessments, decisions, and actions. This rests on a holistic view of the enterprise's security state, the continuous, cross-vendor picture of identities, cloud and infrastructure, data, applications, and their configurations that security posture management assembles. As agents take on this work, what matters is not whether an agent can produce an answer but whether it should be trusted to. The field cannot yet answer this question. Real enterprise environments are private, cross-vendor, and deeply correlated, and none is exposed publicly as a shared, queryable target for evaluating such agents end to end. We call this the environment data gap. We present Open Security Benchmark (OSB), a framework that benchmarks agentic AI on this work. OSB surfaces a curated enterprise environment - a frozen, holistic view of the security state - and evaluates posture investigation across two modalities: text-to-SQL over a relational snapshot and each vendor's native API over a served instance of the same environment. Freezing the environment pins the target state as an immutable snapshot and anchors answers to a closed-form ground truth. OSB is built from five components: a data layer, a task and evaluation-set layer, a multi-dimensional scoring layer, a minimal auditable harness, and a bring-your-own path that serves public comparison and private tenant evaluation from one substrate. We instantiate the framework with two identity-security packs and a family of synthetic-organization environment datasets spanning multiple scales, and chart its extension to further posture subdomains, investigation modalities, and defense stages from assessment toward remediation.

</details>


### [68] [RoguePrompt: Dual-Layer Encoding for Self-Reconstruction to Circumvent LLM Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27373)
*Benyamin Tafreshian,Prathamesh Dhake*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RoguePrompt，一种将禁止提示词分段并应用维吉尼亚密码嵌套ROT13双重编码，再配合自然语言重建指令的多阶段越狱流水线。在313个真实硬拒绝提示上测试，实现了93.93%的过滤绕过率、79.02%的重建率和70.18%的执行率，提供了黑盒条件下多阶段越狱攻击各阶段失败的细粒度证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM越狱攻击的评估通常将提示的可见接受、隐藏请求的成功恢复和随后的执行合并为一个聚合的"攻击成功"指标，这导致缺乏关于多阶段提示变换攻击在黑盒交互中具体失败于哪一阶段的可观察证据。作者希望通过分阶段评估来揭示越狱流水线中各个环节的失败模式。

Method: RoguePrompt采用黑盒威胁模型，仅通过API或用户界面访问托管模型。该方法将禁止提示词进行分区（partition），然后应用两层嵌套编码：维吉尼亚密码（Vigenere）后接ROT13，并附带自然语言重建指令。成功率按三个阶段分别衡量：1）适度性绕过（moderation bypass）、2）指令重建（reconstruction）、3）执行（execution），每个阶段超过其自动化标准即计为成功。

Result: 在313个真实世界硬拒绝提示上测试，RoguePrompt实现了平均93.93%的过滤绕过率、79.02%的重建率和70.18%的执行率，验证了分层提示编码的有效性，同时提供了多阶段越狱在适度性绕过、指令重建和执行各环节失败的阶段级证据。

Conclusion: RoguePrompt证明了分层提示编码（维吉尼亚+ROT13）在黑盒条件下对主流LLM的安全机制具有显著威胁。通过分阶段度量的方式，该工作为理解和改进LLM安全防护在越狱流水线不同环节的薄弱点提供了参考依据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming increasingly integrated into mainstream development platforms and daily technological workflows, typically behind moderation and safety controls. Despite these controls, preventing prompt-based policy evasion remains challenging, and adversaries continue to "jailbreak" LLMs by crafting prompts that circumvent implemented safety mechanisms. Prior work has established cipher-mediated interaction, code-embedded decryption, prompt decomposition and reconstruction, and layered custom encryption as viable attack primitives. However, reported evaluations generally collapse visible acceptance, successful recovery of the concealed request, and subsequent execution into an aggregate attack-success outcome. This leaves limited evidence about where multistage prompt-transformation attacks fail within an observable black-box interaction. This paper introduces RoguePrompt, a jailbreak pipeline that partitions a forbidden prompt and applies two nested encodings, Vigenere followed by ROT13, along with natural-language reconstruction instructions. RoguePrompt was developed and evaluated under a black-box threat model, with only API or user-interface access to the hosted models, and was tested on 313 real-world, hard-rejected prompts. Success was measured in terms of moderation bypass, instruction reconstruction, and execution when the relevant stage exceeded its automated criterion. RoguePrompt achieved average rates of 93.93% for filter bypass, 79.02% for reconstruction, and 70.18% for execution. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of layered prompt encoding while providing stage-level evidence of where multistage jailbreaks fail during moderation bypass, instruction reconstruction, and execution.

</details>


### [69] [Low-Latency Bootstrapping for CKKS using Roots of Unity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27401)
*Jean-Sebastien Coron,Robin Koestler*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SPRU（Sparse Roots of Unity）bootstrapping算法，这是针对CKKS同态加密方案的一种新引导方法。与原始CKKS引导依赖多项式近似模约减不同，SPRU直接将模q的加法群嵌入复数单位根中，利用CKKS方案原生支持复数单位根评估的特性。该方法显著降低了引导所需的乘法深度，从而允许使用更小的环维度，提升整体效率。在OpenFHE C++库中实现时，对少量槽位的密文，SPRU引导可实现最多5倍的延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始CKKS引导方法需要同态评估一个多项式来近似模q约减操作，这导致乘法深度较高，进而需要使用较大的环维度，影响整体计算效率。作者希望通过一种新的引导策略来降低乘法深度，提高CKKS方案的实际可用性。

Method: SPRU引导的关键创新在于不再用多项式近似模约减，而是将模q的加法群直接嵌入到复数单位根（roots of unity）中。由于CKKS方案天然支持对复数单位根的运算，因此可以在同态域中直接评估该嵌入映射，从而避免高深度的多项式近似。这一策略大幅减少了乘法深度，使得可以采用更小的环维度进行引导。

Result: 在OpenFHE C++库中实现了SPRU引导算法，实验结果表明，对于包含少量槽位的密文，SPRU引导相比原始CKKS引导方法可实现最高5倍的延迟降低。

Conclusion: SPRU bootstrapping通过将模q加法群嵌入复数单位根的思路，绕过了高代价的多项式模约减近似，显著降低了CKKS引导的乘法深度和计算延迟，为低槽位密文场景下的同态加密应用提供了一种更高效的引导方案。

Abstract: We introduce Sparse Roots of Unity (SPRU) bootstrapping, a new bootstrapping algorithm for the CKKS homomorphic encryption scheme for approximate arithmetic. The original CKKS bootstrapping method relies on homomorphically evaluating a polynomial that approximates modular reduction modulo q. In contrast, SPRU bootstrapping directly embeds the additive group modulo q into the complex roots of unity, which can be evaluated natively in the CKKS scheme. This approach significantly reduces the multiplicative depth required for bootstrapping, enabling the use of a smaller ring dimension and improving efficiency. In practice, using the OpenFHE C++ library, SPRU bootstrapping achieves up to a 5x reduction in latency when applied to ciphertexts with a small number of slots.

</details>


### [70] [Granite: A Modular Methodology for Foundational Verification of Hardware-Software Leakage Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27480)
*Stella Lau,Andres Erbsen,Adam Chlipala*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Granite是一种针对RTL处理器进行模块化验证的方法论，同时验证功能正确性和非泄漏性，针对ISA契约。它证明了流水线RISC设计（含推测、精确中断和I/O）的逐周期时序仅由ISA泄漏契约中指定的可观察量决定，从而为遵循密码学常数时间纪律的程序排除了通过已知和未知时序侧信道的信息泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件处理器在微架构层面存在各种时序侧信道，如推测执行、精确中断和I/O操作导致的时序差异可能泄漏秘密信息。现有方法要么缺乏模块化，要么无法在指令集架构（ISA）层面与微架构层面的逐周期执行之间建立基础性连接。需要一种方法能够在正确性和机密性上同时提供可组合、可模块化的保证。

Method: Granite的核心技术是"通过确定性实现的泄漏感知精化"（leakage-aware refinement via determinism），即通过与一族逐周期、确定性的规范机器进行迹等价来同时确立正确性和机密性。秘密无关的非确定性通过用不受信任的、仅作用于公开数据的确定性函数对规范进行存在量化来处理。子模块针对各自的泄漏感知规范进行证明，这些证明组合成整体设计保证。所有中间规范（包括ISA契约本身）可从可信计算基中消除。

Result: 首先实现了指令集级泄漏契约与微架构特定逐周期执行（含线级观察）之间的模块化、基础性连接。证明与经过认证的静态分析组合，从硬件和软件密码学实现推导出关于逐周期机密性的单一Rocq定理，消除了包括ISA契约在内的所有中间规范。

Conclusion: Granite证明了在规格仅约束功能正确性和信息流依赖（不约束指令周期数、中断处理时刻或子模块精确延迟）的前提下，可以保证流水线RISC处理器的时序行为仅依赖于公开可观察量，从而消除了已知和未知时序侧信道。

Abstract: Granite is a methodology for modular verification of both functional correctness and nonleakage of RTL processors against ISA contracts. We prove that the cycle-by-cycle timing of a pipelined RISC design--with speculation, precise interrupts, and I/O--is determined solely by observables specified in an ISA leakage contract. For programs that keep observables independent of secrets (i.e., following the cryptographic-constant-time discipline), this result rules out information leakage through known and unknown timing side channels.
  Granite's specifications only constrain functional correctness and information-flow dependencies: not how many cycles an instruction takes, at which instruction an interrupt is handled, or the exact latencies of submodules such as multipliers and memory. Granite's central technique is leakage-aware refinement via determinism, which establishes correctness and confidentiality together as trace equivalence with respect to a family of cycle-level, deterministic spec machines. Secret-independent nondeterminism is handled by existentially parameterizing specs with untrusted, deterministic functions acting only on public data. Submodules are proved against their own leakage-aware specs, and these proofs compose into the whole-design guarantee--which therefore holds over a space of secure implementations.
  We believe this work is the first to achieve modular and foundational connection between instruction-set-level leakage contracts and microarchitecture-specific cycle-by-cycle execution with wire-level observations. Our proofs compose with a certified static analysis that recognizes cryptographic-constant-time code to derive a single Rocq theorem about the cycle-by-cycle confidentiality of a hardware-and-software cryptographic implementation--eliminating every intermediate specification, including the ISA contract itself, from the trusted computing base.

</details>


### [71] [Flock: Fast Proving for Batch Boolean Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27491)
*Benedikt Bünz,Ron D. Rothblum,William Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Flock，一个基于哈希的SNARK系统，用于极快速地证明批量布尔计算（如SHA-256、Keccak、BLAKE3等标准密码学哈希函数的大规模批量求值）。在单核M4 Max处理器上，Flock每秒可证明82k次BLAKE3压缩、42k次SHA-256压缩和30k次Keccak置换，开销低于原生执行的250倍；相比Binius64快9倍以上，相比最快的椭圆曲线SNARK快500倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多SNARK应用中，证明大规模批量标准密码学哈希函数（如SHA-256、Keccak、BLAKE3）的求值是一个关键瓶颈。传统方法在证明这类布尔电路计算时性能受限，亟需更高效的证明系统。

Method: Flock的核心方法包括：(1) 证明同一R1CS电路的批处理（外加输入/输出关系），支持哈希链和Merkle路径展开证明；(2) 对lincheck和zerocheck协议进行新的优化；(3) 与编码智能体共同设计的高优化的概念验证实现。

Result: 在M4 Max单核上：每秒82k次BLAKE3压缩函数求值、42k次SHA-256压缩、30k次Keccak置换，开销小于原生执行的250倍。十核上吞吐量超过每秒660k次BLAKE3压缩。证明SHA-256时比Binius64快9倍以上，比最快的椭圆曲线SNARK快500倍以上。

Conclusion: Flock在批量哈希求值证明方面取得了显著的性能突破，大幅超越了现有最先进方案，证明了通过优化lincheck/zerocheck协议结合协同设计实现，可以实现接近原生执行效率的批量布尔电路SNARK证明。

Abstract: For many applications of SNARKs, a key bottleneck is proving large batches of standard cryptographic hash evaluations, such as SHA-256, Keccak, or BLAKE3. We introduce Flock, a hash-based SNARK for extremely fast proving of such batched Boolean computations. Flock proves batches of the same R1CS circuit (plus input/output relations between them), can prove hash-chains and Merkle path openings, and in principle can be extended to full-fledged hash-based signature verification. At its core, Flock combines new optimizations for the lincheck and zerocheck protocols with an aggressively optimized proof-of-concept implementation co-designed by coding agents.
  On a single core of an M4 Max processor, Flock proves 82k evaluations of the BLAKE3 compression function, 42k SHA-256 compressions, and 30k Keccak permutations per second --- less than a $250\times$ overhead over native execution. On ten cores, throughput exceeds 660k BLAKE3 compressions per second; in proving SHA-256, Flock is more than $9\times$ faster than Binius64, the prior state of the art, and more than $500\times$ faster than the fastest elliptic curve-based SNARK we measured against.

</details>


### [72] [Send and Pretend: Exploiting Transcript Consistency Issues in End-to-End Encrypted Group Chats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27510)
*Gabriel K. Gegenhuber,Moritz Grefner,Maximilian Günther,Matthäus Wininger,David Schmidt,Aljosha Judmayer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地证明当前主流端到端加密（E2EE）即时通讯应用（如Signal、iMessage、WhatsApp、Threema等）在群聊场景下均无法保证"会话一致性"（Transcript Consistency），恶意群成员可以针对不同接收者选择性隐瞒、重排或篡改消息内容而不触发用户界面警告，并可实施投票操纵（rigging polls）、社会工程攻击、规避审核等具体攻击。文章识别了多个歧义发送（equivocation）向量，涵盖协议回退路径及群组内点对点投递渠道，还发现了与隐私相关的实现特定行为（如设备操作系统指纹）。最后给出了低开销的缓解措施和UI信号策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E2EE即时通讯应用被广泛用于敏感群组协作（如政治决策），但主流应用新引入的投票功能隐式假设所有参与者看到一致的会话视图（即会话一致性TC）。然而这一关键安全属性在群聊场景中是否成立从未被系统验证，作者旨在揭示这一安全与信任假设的漏洞。

Method: 作者在恶意参与者威胁模型下对主流E2EE通讯应用进行系统性调查，该模型以共享会话完整性以及用户多设备间不一致投递为目标。他们识别出从协议回退路径到群组内点对点投递渠道的多种歧义发送向量，并通过具体攻击场景（社会工程、规避审核、操纵投票等）进行实证演示，同时结合实现层面的隐私泄露行为（如设备OS指纹）进行分析，最终将发现置于既有会话一致性研究框架中进行语境化。

Result: 研究发现所有被调查的主流E2EE即时通讯应用在群聊中均不保证任何形式的会话一致性，恶意群成员可实现选择性消息投递、乱序或内容篡改且不被用户界面检测到。作者成功演示了投票操纵、社会工程、规避审核等现实攻击，并发现跨服务的设计缺陷以及实现特定的隐私问题（如通过消息机制泄露设备OS信息）。

Conclusion: 主流E2EE群协议缺乏会话一致性保障是一个普遍且严重的系统性缺陷，但可以通过低开销的协议修改和UI信号策略来缓解。作者提出的缓解措施可集成到当前E2EE群组协议中，以改善群聊场景下的一致性与安全性。

Abstract: End-to-end encrypted (E2EE) messaging apps are widely praised for their security and thus also used for sensitive coordination in group chats (e.g., by political decision makers). After Threema and WhatsApp, also Signal and iMessage have recently introduced polls to aid agreement processes in groups. This implicitly sets the expectation that all participants see the same outcome and thus have the same view of the conversation. This property is commonly referred to as transcript consistency (TC). In this work, we demonstrate that today's major E2EE messengers do not guarantee any form of TC for group chats, allowing a malicious group member to selectively omit, reorder, or present altered content to different recipients without triggering warnings in their user interface. We systematically investigate the extent of the problem under a malicious-participant threat model that targets the integrity of the shared transcript, or inconsistent delivery across a user's linked devices. We identify multiple equivocation vectors that range from protocol fallback paths to deliberate use of pairwise delivery channels within groups. We demonstrate concrete exploitation scenarios such as social engineering, evading moderation, and, in particular, rigging polls. Beyond these cross-service design issues, we also uncover implementation-specific behaviors with privacy implications (e.g., device OS fingerprinting). Finally, we contextualize our findings within prior transcript-consistency research and outline practical low-overhead mitigations and UI signaling strategies that can be integrated into state-of-the-art E2EE group protocols.

</details>


### [73] [ThreatForest: Multi-Agent Attack Tree Generation with Pluggable TTP Framework Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27528)
*Cristian Leo,Anton Dykyi,Danny Cortegaca,Daniel Begimher,Prakash Jha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ThreatForest是一个多智能体系统，能够从源码仓库自动生成结构化攻击树，将攻击步骤映射到多种对抗框架的战术、技术和程序（TTP），并合成可操作的缓解措施。系统采用多阶段流水线架构并引入人工验证点，评估显示威胁语句、攻击树和缓解措施质量较高（0.63-0.68），但嵌入模型驱动的TTP映射是主要精度瓶颈（仅0.29），且该瓶颈来自编码器本身而非多智能体设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生架构的威胁建模依赖人工分析，过程缓慢且需要稀缺的安全专家资源。现有方法难以自动化地从代码仓库规模地生成结构化的威胁模型，尤其是跨多个对抗框架的TTP映射和缓解措施合成。

Method: 多智能体流水线：仓库分析→上下文精炼→威胁生成→并行攻击树构建（含TTP映射与缓解合成）→报告生成，以有向图编排并配备确定性验证门、有限重试和三个人类介入验证点。使用领域特定的sentence-transformer通过余弦相似度将攻击步骤映射到候选技术。

Result: 在七个应用域和一个十六维度评分规则下，由独立LLM评审团（含对抗性验证和专家审查）评估，威胁语句、攻击树和缓解措施的质量达到0.63-0.68（0-1尺度），但仅嵌入的TTP映射仅为0.29。受控单次调用基线将映射可辩护性提高了一倍以上，证明瓶颈在于嵌入编码器而非多智能体设计。

Conclusion: ThreatForest是首个将代码仓库转化为跨对抗框架、带TTP映射攻击树和基于证据缓解措施的端到端系统，同时提供了一个可复用的基准测试框架。核心发现是领域嵌入编码器是当前系统精度的主导瓶颈。

Abstract: Threat modeling is essential for secure software development, yet manual analysis of cloud-native architectures is slow and demands scarce security expertise. We present ThreatForest, a multi-agent system that generates structured attack trees from source code repositories, maps attack steps to adversary tactics, techniques, and procedures (TTPs) from a pluggable set of frameworks (MITRE ATT&CK, CAPEC, and cloud-specific threat matrices), and synthesizes actionable mitigations. ThreatForest decomposes threat modeling into a multi-stage agent pipeline -- repository analysis, context refinement, threat generation, parallel attack-tree construction with TTP mapping and mitigation synthesis, and report generation -- orchestrated as a directed graph with deterministic verification gates, bounded retries, and three human-in-the-loop validation points. A domain-specific sentence-transformer maps each attack step to candidate techniques by cosine similarity; we show empirically that this embedding stage, not the surrounding pipeline, is the dominant accuracy bottleneck. We evaluate ThreatForest across seven application domains on a sixteen-dimension rubric, scored by a panel of independent LLM raters with an adversarial verification pass and expert review. Panel-measured quality reaches 0.63-0.68 (on a 0-1 scale) for threat statements, attack trees, and mitigations, but only 0.29 for embedding-only TTP mapping -- a gap stable across all seven domains that isolates the binding constraint. A controlled single-call baseline on the same model more than doubles mapping defensibility, pinning the limitation on the embedding encoder rather than the multi-agent design. To our knowledge, ThreatForest is the first end-to-end system that turns a code repository into TTP-mapped attack trees with evidence-based mitigations across adversary frameworks, with a reusable framework for benchmarking such systems.

</details>


### [74] [AnchorMark: Robust Diffusion Watermarking via Latent-Space Rotation Synchrony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27551)
*Yuqi Qian,Yun Cao,Haocheng Fu,Haochen Zhao,Hong Zhang,Meineng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AnchorMark是一种无需训练的、鲁棒的基于反转（inversion）的水印方法，利用潜在空间中"旋转同步性"特性，通过在初始潜在空间的中心区域嵌入同步锚点来估计和校正旋转角，从而在旋转及复合损失性后处理攻击下显著提升比特准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于反转的水印方法在复合有损后处理下仍然脆弱，尤其是涉及旋转攻击时，旋转会破坏潜在空间解码所需的空域对应关系，导致解码失效。

Method: 发现了潜在空间中的"旋转同步性"特性，即图像域的旋转与恢复的初始潜变量中的对应变换共享相同角度。基于此，AnchorMark在初始潜变量的中央区域嵌入同步锚点，利用该锚点在提取阶段准确估计并校正旋转角度。

Result: 实验表明AnchorMark在旋转攻击及复合攻击下显著提高了比特准确率，同时对图像质量影响有限。

Conclusion: AnchorMark通过无需训练的设计和同步锚点机制，有效解决了基于反转水印在旋转条件下的脆弱性问题，为鲁棒水印提供了一种高效且实用的方案。

Abstract: Inversion-based watermarking embeds watermark payloads directly into the generative process, avoiding a separate post-hoc image-domain embedding stage while preserving the native visual fidelity of synthesized images. However, existing methods remain vulnerable to compound lossy post-processing, particularly when rotation is involved, as it disrupts the spatial correspondence required for latent-space decoding. To overcome this limitation, we introduce AnchorMark, a training-free, robust inversion-based watermarking. We uncover a latent-space property termed Rotation Synchrony: image-domain rotations and their counterparts in the recovered initial latent share the same angle. Building on this property, AnchorMark embeds a synchronization anchor in the central region of the initial latent, enabling accurate estimation and correction of the rotation angle during extraction. Experiments show that AnchorMark substantially improves bit accuracy under rotation and combined attacks, with limited impact on image quality.

</details>


### [75] [Revisiting the Adversarial Robustness of Graph-Based Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27604)
*Qingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了图神经网络交通流量预测的对抗鲁棒性问题，指出现有攻击与防御基于不切实际的威胁模型。作者提出一种物理感知的定位攻击（针对特定路段误导预估到达时间或引发绕路），并发现对抗训练在此情境下效果不佳。为此，他们将鲁棒性重构为检测问题，引入物理信息感知的检测器，其输出作为特征融入加固后的预测器，在与自适应攻击对抗的评估中，该方法在15个模型-数据集组合中的13个上超越了包括针对该攻击本身进行对抗训练在内的所有基线，且干净样本性能几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通流量预测的对抗鲁棒性研究建立在不现实的威胁模型和不明确的攻击目标之上。实际攻击者只具备有限的模型知识，仅能操控少量道路传感器，且攻击应精准定位于特定路段或线路，造成误报到达时间或不必要绕路的同时保持网络整体影响最小化。这种"定位攻击"场景未得到充分探索，且现有防御（如对抗训练）无法从训练的范数有界攻击泛化到结构不同的物理感知攻击。

Method: 将鲁棒性重新界定为检测问题：设计一个学习型的物理信息感知检测器（physics-informed detector），其输出作为附加输入特征馈送给一个加固的预测器（forecaster），并在预测器固定的条件下针对自适应攻击进行联合对抗训练。攻击侧则构建一种符合真实拥堵物理规律的定位攻击，使目标链路的预测误差成倍增大而全局误差几乎不变。

Result: 物理感知攻击可使目标链路误差成倍增大，同时网络全局误差几乎无变化；针对范数有界扰动调优的对抗训练对此类攻击几乎无效。所提出的检测-缓解防御在15个模型-数据集组合中的13个上优于甚至超过专门针对物理感知攻击训练的对抗训练，在保留攻击（held-out attack）上优势最大，且干净样本性能代价接近为零。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在应用特定约束下审视抽象化AI对抗攻击的重要性，以评估其真实安全影响。物理感知的定位攻击暴露了现有鲁棒性评估的盲区，而将鲁棒性重构为检测-缓解范式比单纯对抗训练更为有效且更具泛化性。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting by graph-based AI is a critical component of intelligent transportation systems, motivating security research on robustness to malicious sensor readings. We argue that prior robustness evaluations are largely shaped by unrealistic threat models and untargeted objectives, so both attacks and defenses must be revisited. We study a practical adversary with limited model knowledge and the ability to monitor and manipulate only a few road sensors. More importantly, practical attacks can be localized to specific links or routes, causing incorrect estimated arrival times or unnecessary rerouting while leaving the broader network largely unaffected. This targeted setting remains underexplored, and defenses such as adversarial training do not transfer well from the norm-bounded attacks they train on to structurally different, physics-aware attacks that mimic genuine congestion. We therefore reframe robustness as a detection problem, introducing a learned physics-informed detector whose output is fed to a hardened forecaster as an input feature and trained against adaptive attacks with the forecaster fixed. We evaluate across a variety of model architectures and benchmarks. The physics-aware attack multiplies target-link error several-fold while the network-wide error barely moves, and adversarial training, tuned to norm-bounded perturbations, barely dents it. Our detection--mitigation defense improves even on adversarial training hardened against the physics-aware attack itself, on $13$ of $15$ model--dataset settings and by the widest margin on a held-out attack, at near-zero clean cost. The results emphasize the need to examine abstracted AI adversarial attacks under application-specific constraints to assess their true security impacts.

</details>


### [76] [Strategy Phasing of Cyber Attacks on Digital Substations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27661)
*Akila Herath,Chen-Ching Liu,Junho Hong,Kuchan Park*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SubCASP，一种基于隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）的变电站网络攻击策略分阶段推断方法，通过融合多个入侵检测系统（IDS）的数据日志，推断当前攻击阶段、预测下一攻击阶段并回溯攻击路径，以支持针对多阶段协同攻击的缓解措施部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符合IEC 61850标准的数字变电站虽提高了运行效率，但攻击者可利用IEC 61850通信操纵断路器操作，造成严重系统影响。这些网络攻击基于多阶段策略构建，而现有IDS仅能标记孤立的攻击症状，缺乏攻击阶段上下文，无法为缓解措施的部署提供支持。

Method: SubCASP采用隐马尔可夫模型（HMM），融合多个IDS的数据日志来推断攻击阶段序列。攻击阶段基于ATT&CK威胁建模框架推导得出。模型在可复现的攻击图数据集上进行训练和评估，并测试了不同IDS可观测性级别和缺失IDS数据日志场景下的鲁棒性。

Result: 测试结果表明，SubCASP在不同IDS可观测性级别和IDS数据日志缺失场景下均表现出鲁棒性，能够有效推断当前攻击阶段、预测下一阶段并回溯攻击路径。

Conclusion: SubCASP为变电站网络攻击提供了具备阶段上下文的检测能力，弥补了传统IDS缺乏攻击阶段信息的不足，有助于在攻击的不同阶段部署针对性的缓解措施。

Abstract: Digital substations that comply with IEC 61850 have improved the operational efficiency of modern power systems. However, adversaries can abuse IEC 61850 communication to manipulate circuit breaker operations in substations, which can result in severe system impacts. These cyber attacks are crafted based on broader multi-phase strategies. The existing intrusion detection systems (IDSs) often flag only isolated symptoms. Thus, there is a lack of context in the attack phase to support the deployment of mitigation measures. This paper proposes Substation Cyber Attack Strategy Phasing (SubCASP), a Hidden Markov Model(HMM)- based method that fuses IDS data logs to infer the current attack phase, next attack phase, and retrospective attack path. The attack phases are derived from an ATT&CK-based threat modeling. The SubCASP model is trained and evaluated on a reproducible attack-graph dataset. Test results are presented to demonstrate the robustness of SubCASP for various IDS observability levels and missing IDS data logs scenarios.

</details>


### [77] [Distributed Point Functions and Function Secret Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27696)
*Elette Boyle,Niv Gilboa,Yuval Ishai,Peter Scholl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于分布式点函数（DPF）的综述文章，涵盖其定义、构造和应用，并讨论了其向函数秘密共享（FSS）的扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPF作为一种轻量级密码原语，在众多应用中展现出强大的实用性（如私有信息检索、匿名消息传递、安全计算和伪随机相关生成器），需要一个系统性的综述来梳理其定义、构造方法和应用场景。

Method: 作为一篇综述论文，系统性地总结和分类DPF的定义、构造方法及其应用，并进一步讨论DPF到FSS的泛化扩展，说明如何将点函数推广到更丰富的函数类别。

Result: 完成了对DPF定义、构造和应用的全面综述，并展示了高效的FSS方案能够为大多数DPF应用提供类似的推广。

Conclusion: DPF的轻量级结构使其成为多种密码应用的基础构建模块，且通过扩展到FSS，可以支持更丰富的函数类别，从而扩展其应用范围。

Abstract: A distributed point function (DPF) is a cryptographic primitive that enables compressed additive sharing of a secret weight-1 vector (equivalently, a point function) across two or more parties. The appealing lightweight structure of DPF constructions has enabled a wide range of applications. These include private information retrieval, anonymous messaging, secure computation with preprocessing, and pseudorandom correlation generators for expanding small correlated seeds into large pseudorandom instances of cryptographic correlations.
  In this article, we survey definitions, constructions, and applications of DPFs. We also discuss the extension of DPF to function secret sharing (FSS), which generalizes point functions to support richer function classes. Efficient FSS schemes yield a similar generalization for most of the applications of DPFs.

</details>


### [78] [CHARGE: Leveraging CWE Hierarchies for Hardware Security SystemVerilog Assertion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27776)
*Xiao Tan,Cynthia Sturton*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CHARGE，一个利用CWE层级结构和大型语言模型自动生成RTL模块安全属性的框架，无需可信设计规格，在Hack@DAC18/19/21 SoC设计上检测出27/42已知漏洞，并发现1个新漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证RTL模块的安全性通常需要人工编写属性和依赖可信的设计规格，耗时费力且容易出错。现有的自动方法缺乏对安全关键资产的准确识别能力，因此需要一种自动化框架来降低人工成本并提高安全属性生成的准确性。

Method: CHARGE利用CWE条目的层级结构作为推理基础，引导LLM识别未验证RTL模块中的安全关键资产，再从资产和CWE语义中推断预期的安全行为并生成SystemVerilog Assertions (SVAs)。整个过程无需可信设计规格，并通过CWE层次关系提升资产识别的准确性。

Result: 在Hack@DAC18、19、21的SoC设计上使用GPT-4.1评估，CHARGE检测出42个已知漏洞中的27个。在Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC上，89%生成的SVA可在Cadence JasperGold FPV中运行，92.2%为非空断言。与手工编写的开源属性相比，CHARGE在3个漏洞上正确编写了手工属性错误的属性，并发现了1个此前未被识别的新漏洞。

Conclusion: CHARGE展示了利用CWE层级结构结合LLM自动生成安全属性的有效性，减少了对可信规格和人工工程的依赖，并证明了其在发现未知漏洞方面的潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents CHARGE, an automated framework for generating security properties for unverified RTL modules using CWEs and large language models (LLMs). The hallmark is a reasoning process that leverages the hierarchical nature of CWE entries to improve accuracy when identifying security-critical assets in unverified RTL modules. As a result, the approach can infer expected security behaviors and generate properties from identified assets and CWE semantics, avoiding the need for trusted design specifications and reducing manual engineering effort. We evaluate the framework on the Hack@DAC18, 19, and 21 open source SoC designs using OpenAI's GPT-4.1. CHARGE detects 27 of 42 known bugs in these designs. For Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC, 89% of the generated SVAs run in Cadence JasperGold FPV, and 92.2% are non-vacuous. We compare to an open-source, manually written set of properties for these designs and find that CHARGE correctly writes properties for three bugs in which the manually written properties were incorrect. In addition, CHARGE-generated properties identify a new bug in the Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC that was not previously identified.

</details>


### [79] [Adaptive Security at the Edge for 6G-Enabled Healthcare IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27858)
*Ijaz Ahmad,Ijaz Ahmad,Erkki Harjula*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: NANOEDGEGUARD是一个内核态闭环控制器，在边缘网关处观察每源流量强度并执行可审计的多层速率策略。在Raspberry Pi网关与MQTT/ESP32测试平台上，相比用户态防火墙基线，99分位告警RTT降低13.3%，超量突发流量降低46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗物联网服务依赖边缘网关转发常规遥测数据和稀有时效性告警，但短时流量突发会膨胀最坏情况延迟并干扰紧急消息，现有静态防火墙规则或用户态控制回路无法及时响应。

Method: 采用内核平面（kernel-plane）闭环控制器，通过内核流量控制钩子（in-kernel traffic-control hooks）观测每源流量强度，执行可审计的多层速率策略；利用迟滞机制（hysteresis）兼顾快速动作与显式恢复，并记录策略转换日志以支持审计。测试平台为Raspberry Pi网关承载MQTT broker，配两个ESP32端点产生生命体征、告警及定时突发流量。

Result: 相比用户态防火墙基线，99分位告警RTT降低13.3%且维持无强制控制的RTT水平；相比无强制控制，超量准入突发流量减少46%。

Conclusion: 自适应、可审计的网关强制策略可提升医疗物联网韧性，未来可扩展至按需策略部署的边缘智能场景。

Abstract: Healthcare IoT services increasingly rely on edge gateways to relay routine telemetry and deliver rare but timecritical alarms. Even short traffic bursts can inflate worstcase delay and interfere with urgent messages. We present NANOEDGEGUARD, a kernel-plane closed-loop controller that observes per-source traffic intensity at the edge and enforces an auditable, multi-tier rate policy using in-kernel traffic-control hooks. Unlike static firewall rules or user-space control loops, our design prioritizes fast actuation and explicit recovery through hysteresis, and it records policy transitions for auditability. Using a Raspberry Pi gateway hosting an MQTT broker and two ESP32 endpoints generating vitals, alarms, and a timed burst, we show that adaptive kernel-plane rate control reduces the 99th-percentile alarm RTT by 13.3% compared to a user-space firewall baseline while maintaining no-enforcement-level RTT, and it reduces excess admitted burst traffic by 46% compared to no enforcement. These early results indicate that adaptive, auditable enforcement at the gateway can improve resilience for healthcare IoT, and it can be extended toward on-demand policy deployment in future edge intelligence.

</details>


### [80] [Don't Trust the AI Ecosystem: Analyzing Privacy Leakage in Compromised Open-Source Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27886)
*Jin-Seong Kim,Han-Ju Lee,Seok-Won Hong,Takeshi Takahashi,Chansu Han,Tomohiro Morikawa,Seok-Hwan Choi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GradLock是一种新型训练时注入攻击方法，通过静态确定性索引和动态梯度锁机制将敏感训练数据直接嵌入模型参数中，在不保留训练环境访问权限的情况下实现像素级精确的数据提取。在MNIST、Imagenette和CelebA上实现近无损重建（SSIM≈1.0）和即时提取（<1.0s），且对量化、剪枝和微调等部署优化具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型反演（MI）攻击主要依赖训练后优化来从模型输出恢复私有数据，但这些方法受限于目标模型的泛化瓶颈，往往只能恢复通用特征而非特定身份，尤其在高维数据集上效果不佳。因此需要一种能够在训练阶段直接注入敏感数据的新攻击范式。

Method: GradLock采用三个核心机制：(1) 在受损供应链环境中进行训练时注入攻击，将敏感数据直接嵌入模型参数；(2) 使用无状态确定性索引建立隔离的数据保险库（isolated data vaults）；(3) 引入动态梯度锁（dynamic gradient locking）防止有效载荷在优化过程中退化。攻击者在最终模型上即可提取像素级精确数据，无需保留训练环境访问权限。

Result: 在MNIST、Imagenette和CelebA数据集上，GradLock实现近无损重建（SSIM≈1.0）和即时提取（<1.0s）。相比现有训练时注入方法，对量化、剪枝和微调等标准部署优化表现出更强鲁棒性。用户部署研究中93.3%的参与者未能检测到恶意逻辑。

Conclusion: GradLock揭示了现代AI供应链中训练时注入攻击的严重安全盲区，在模型部署的常见优化手段下仍能保持高度隐蔽性和攻击有效性，凸显了对AI供应链安全进行系统性防护的紧迫性。

Abstract: Existing model inversion (MI) attacks predominantly rely on post-training optimization to recover private data from model outputs. However, these methods are fundamentally constrained by the target model's generalization bottleneck, often yielding generic features rather than specific identities, particularly on high-dimensional datasets. In this paper, we introduce GradLock, a novel training-time injection attack that stealthily injects sensitive training data directly into the model parameters. Operating within a compromised supply chain context, GradLock leverages stateless deterministic indexing to establish isolated data vaults and employs dynamic gradient locking to prevent payload degradation during the optimization process. This mechanism allows the adversary to extract pixel-perfect data from the final model without retaining access to the training environment. Extensive experiments on MNIST, Imagenette, and CelebA demonstrate that GradLock achieves near-lossless reconstruction (SSIM ~ 1.0) and instant extraction (< 1.0s). Compared to existing training-time injection methods, our approach exhibits superior robustness against standard deployment optimizations, including quantization, pruning, and fine-tuning. Furthermore, a user deployment study reveals that 93.3% of participants failed to detect the malicious logic, highlighting a severe blind spot in the security of modern AI supply chains.

</details>


### [81] [Benign on Label, Malicious by Design: Clean-Label Dormant-to-Activated Backdoor via Machine Unlearning with Removable Camouflage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27936)
*Dongdong Zhao,Can Li,Xiang Yao,Fan He,Qihang Ge,Baogang Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于双生成器学习的clean-label反遗忘激活后门框架，通过将问题建模为双层优化，交替学习建立潜在触发-目标关联的样本特定触发器与提供可移除抑制的标签一致伪装样本。当少量伪装样本被遗忘后，抑制被解除，休眠后门被激活。在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-10上的实验表明该方法在多个遗忘算法下均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击通常在后门植入后立即生效，可能在利用之前就暴露。机器遗忘激活的休眠后门通过在训练后保持非活跃、仅在部分训练记录被遗忘后才生效来缓解行为暴露。然而，现有方法难以在clean-label约束和现实遗忘请求下同时实现低遗忘前攻击成功率和强遗忘后激活，因为实现这一转换需要联合建立持久的潜在关联和可移除的抑制影响。

Method: 提出clean-label遗忘激活后门框架，基于双生成器学习并建模为双层优化问题：通过模拟潜在后门建立和机器遗忘过程，交替学习(1)建立潜在触发到目标关联的样本特定触发器，以及(2)提供可移除抑制的标签一致伪装样本。当少量伪装样本被遗忘后，抑制被解除，休眠后门被激活。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-10数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多种遗忘算法下，相较于代表性后门基线，能维持更低的遗忘前攻击成功率，同时实现更强的遗忘后激活效果。这证明了通过协调持久潜在关联与可移除抑制，可以在clean-label和现实删除约束下实现可靠的休眠到激活的转换。

Conclusion: 通过将持久潜在关联与可移除抑制相协调，可以在clean-label和现实遗忘约束下实现可靠的休眠到激活转换，为机器遗忘激活后门攻击提供了系统化的框架设计。

Abstract: Existing backdoor attacks often become effective immediately after backdoor implantation and may therefore be exposed before exploitation. Machine unlearning activated dormant backdoors mitigate such behavioral exposure by remaining inactive after training and becoming effective only after selected training records are unlearned. However, existing methods struggle to simultaneously achieve a low pre-unlearning attack success rate and strong post-unlearning activation under clean-label constraints and realistic unlearning requests. Achieving this transition requires jointly establishing a persistent latent association and a removable suppressive influence. To address this challenge, we propose a clean-label unlearning-activated backdoor framework based on dual-generator learning and formulate it as a bilevel optimization problem: By simulating latent backdoor establishment and machine unlearning, the framework alternately learns sample-specific triggers that establish a latent trigger-to-target association and label-consistent camouflage samples that provide removable suppression. Once a small subset of camouflage samples is unlearned, the suppression is lifted and the dormant backdoor is activated. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-10 show that our method maintains lower pre-unlearning attack success rates while achieving stronger post-unlearning activation across multiple unlearning algorithms than representative backdoor baselines. These results demonstrate that reliable dormancy-to-activation transitions can be achieved by coordinating a persistent latent association with removable suppression under clean-label and realistic deletion constraints.

</details>


### [82] [Safeguards Based on Copyable Context Cannot Provide Reliable Safety for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27951)
*Pingyu Wu,Lingyao Zhu,Weiming Zhang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出大语言模型的安全防护机制无法在回答前区分有益专业用户与恶意攻击者，并由此推导出"有用能力、可靠安全、开放访问"三者不可兼得的安全三难困境，进而提出通过可信凭证引入难以复制的外部证据来降低恶意利用的下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型安全防护机制在生成回答前无法预知答案的最终用途，这导致双用途（dual-use）任务中同一答案可能被专业人员或攻击者利用。由于攻击者可以模仿合法用户的提问与交互历史，仅靠内容层面的防护无法有效区分两类用户，因此需要从理论上厘清能力释放与用途证据之间的根本矛盾。

Method: 论文将模型释放的能力与下游使用证据进行分离分析。当证据可复制时，推导出攻击者获得最坏情况下协助下限的精确表达式；基于此结果提出安全三难困境。进一步论证可信凭证（trusted credential）可提供难以复制的信息以预测真实下游用途，并确定消除该下限所需的更强条件。

Result: 推导出可复制证据场景下攻击者协助的最坏情况下限，证明了"有用能力、可靠安全、开放访问"三者不可兼得的安全三难困境；表明可信凭证能够有效补充现有防护机制，并识别出彻底消除攻击协助下限所需的更严格的充分条件。来自双用途评估、自适应攻击和已部署可信访问项目的证据支持这些条件的实践有效性。

Conclusion: 大语言模型安全防护的根本局限在于用途不可预知性与证据可复制性之间的矛盾；在开放访问前提下无法同时保证有用能力与可靠安全，需借助可信凭证等外部机制引入不可复制的用途证据来缓解这一根本性冲突。

Abstract: Large language model safeguards decide whether to answer before seeing how an answer will be used. This creates a basic problem for dual-use tasks: the same answer can help an authorized professional or an attacker, while an attacker can imitate a benign request and interaction history. We separate the capability released by the model from the evidence available about downstream use. When that evidence is copyable, we derive the exact worst-case floor on attacker assistance while preserving useful answers. The result yields a safety trilemma: Useful Capability, Reliable Safety, and Open Access cannot coexist. We then show how a trusted credential can complement existing safeguards by adding hard-to-copy information that predicts actual downstream use, and identify the stronger condition needed to eliminate the floor. Evidence from dual-use evaluations, adaptive attacks, and deployed trusted-access programs supports the practical relevance of these conditions.

</details>


### [83] [Driving up Inference Energy on SNNs: Per-Sample and Universal Sponge Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27990)
*Spyridon Raptis,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示脉冲神经网络（SNN）因其稀疏二进制事件通信带来的能效优势，反而引入了新的安全风险——海绵攻击可通过增加推理时的脉冲活动和突触工作量来膨胀能耗，且难以通过正确性监控检测。作者提出两种攻击模型：针对每个输入定制的逐样本海绵攻击（使SynOps增加1.5-2.6倍，同时保持98%以上样本的类别不变），以及首次提出的适用于原生事件驱动SNN输入的通用海绵攻击（通过XOR应用固定二进制扰动，SynOps增加1.09-1.24倍）。映射到Loihi-1硬件能耗，每次推理开销为14μJ到13.24mJ，表明原生事件驱动SNN易受实际输入空间效率攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN在神经形态硬件上通过稀疏二进制尖峰事件实现能效推理，被广泛用于始终在线的电池供电边缘系统。然而，这种能效优势同时带来了独特的安全隐患：攻击者可以增加推理时的尖峰活动和突触计算量，从而膨胀能耗并造成电池快速耗尽，而基于正确性的监控无法有效检测这种攻击。现有的输入空间效率攻击主要聚焦于率编码（rate-coded）设置下的逐样本优化，缺乏针对原生事件驱动二进制输入的攻击研究。

Method: 本文定义了两种攻击模型：（1）逐样本海绵攻击：针对每个输入，通过基于梯度的优化方法定制 adversarial spike train，最大化SynOps（突触操作数）同时保持预测类别不变；（2）通用海绵攻击：首次为原生事件驱动SNN输入设计的一种固定二进制扰动，通过离线计算后以XOR方式应用到所有后续输入，无需逐输入优化。实验使用了NMNIST、SHD和IBM DVS Gesture三个数据集上的三个SNN模型进行验证。

Result: 逐样本海绵攻击在三个SNN模型上使每推理SynOps增加1.5-2.6倍，同时至少98%的样本保持预测类别不变；通用海绵攻击虽效果较弱（SynOps增加1.09-1.24倍），但代表更现实的部署威胁。将SynOp膨胀映射到Loihi-1估计能耗，每次推理的额外开销为14μJ至13.24mJ，表明可重复使用的通用扰动能在持续部署的边缘系统中累积成显著的电池消耗。

Conclusion: 原生事件驱动的SNN容易受到实际的输入空间效率攻击（海绵攻击）影响。即使通用扰动攻击的效果弱于逐样本定制攻击，其无需逐输入优化的特性使其更具实际部署威胁，可能导致持续运行的边缘设备出现有意义的电池消耗累积。这一发现强调了在SNN安全评估中需要考虑能力效率维度的必要性。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) communicate through sparse binary spike events rather than dense activations, enabling energy-efficient inference on neuromorphic hardware and motivating their use in always-on, battery-powered edge systems. We show that this same efficiency advantage creates a distinct security risk: sponge attacks can increase inference-time spike activity and synaptic workload, inflating energy consumption while remaining difficult to detect through correctness-based monitoring alone. Prior input-space efficiency attacks on SNNs have focused on per-sample optimization, primarily in rate-coded settings. We extend this threat to native event-based binary inputs and study two attack models. First, we develop a per-sample sponge attack that crafts a custom adversarial spike train for each input via gradient-based optimization. This attack increases per-inference SynOps by 1.5-2.6x on three SNN models for the NMNIST, SHD, and IBM DVS Gesture datasets, while preserving the predicted class on at least 98% of evaluated samples. Second, to the best of our knowledge, we introduce the first universal sponge attack for native event-based SNN inputs: a fixed binary perturbation computed offline and applied via XOR to all subsequent inputs. Although weaker, it still inflates SynOps by 1.09-1.24x across all three datasets and represents a more realistic deployment threat because it requires no per-input optimization. Mapping SynOp inflation to estimated Loihi-1 energy yields per-inference overheads from 14 $μ$J to 13.24 mJ. These results show that native event-based SNNs are vulnerable to practical input-space efficiency attacks, and that reusable universal perturbations can accumulate into meaningful battery drain in continuously deployed edge systems.

</details>


### [84] [Temporal Poisoning: Clean-Label Backdoors via Event Redistribution in SNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28075)
*Roberto Riaño,Gorka Abad,Stjepan Picek,Aitor Urbieta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出并系统评估了针对脉冲神经网络（SNNs）的干净标签时序后门攻击。该攻击通过对目标类训练流施加固定时间戳变换（保持逐像素逐极性事件计数不变），在不改变标签的情况下实现高达1.00的ASR。作者分析了攻击在三种神经形态数据集和两种受害者架构上的有效性，并提出了基于逐步骤事件质量的检测器，展示了速率塌缩防御的局限性与攻击隐蔽性的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有SNN后门攻击主要假设脏标签投毒（需对触发样本重新标注到攻击者选定类别），而干净标签攻击（标签不变、更难被察觉）在SNN上尚未被研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索时序域中的干净标签投毒可能性。

Method: 攻击方法：对目标类训练流仅施加固定的时间戳变换，保持标签不变。该变换精确保持逐像素、逐极性的事件计数，使干净样本与触发样本在时间聚合后完全一致，但改变了SNN实际处理的时序序列。实验范围覆盖三种神经形态数据集、卷积与Transformer两类受害者架构；通过投毒预算和触发形状消融分析攻击特性；并提出了基于逐步骤事件质量的模型无关检测器。

Result: 在最强配置下攻击成功率（ASR）达到1.00。时间轴塌缩类防御因构造性盲区无法检测攻击；特征空间方法仅在特定设置下能检测到投毒。作者提出的基于逐步骤事件质量的检测器成功检测了所评估的时序变换攻击。

Conclusion: 该工作首次证明SNN上存在有效的干净标签后门攻击，揭示了时序信息是SNN安全的一个独特且被低估的攻击面。速率塌缩（时间聚合）类防御在对抗此类攻击时天然失效，但攻击的隐蔽性存在边界——逐步骤事件质量检测器可有效识别。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks on Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have primarily assumed dirty-label poisoning, in which triggered training samples are relabeled to an attacker-selected class. We study clean-label temporal poisoning, where a fixed timestamp transformation is applied only to the target-class training streams, leaving their labels unchanged. The transformation preserves the per-pixel, per-polarity event count exactly, making clean and triggered samples identical after temporal aggregation while altering the sequence processed by the SNN. Across three neuromorphic datasets and both convolutional and transformer-based victims, the attack reaches an ASR of 1.00 in the strongest configurations. We analyze the attack through poison-budget and trigger-shape ablations and evaluate established backdoor defenses adapted to spiking models. Defenses that collapse the time axis before inspection are blind by construction, while feature-space methods detect the poison only in selected settings. Our model-free detector, based on per-step event mass, detects the evaluated temporal transformations, demonstrating both the limitation of rate-collapsed defenses and the boundary of the attack's stealth. To our knowledge, this is the first clean-label backdoor attack evaluated on SNNs and neuromorphic event data.

</details>


### [85] [Checking Information Flow in Cloud-based IoT Access Control Policies (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28088)
*Lorenzo Ceragioli,Letterio Galletta,Edoardo Lunati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析物联网云平台（AWS IoT Core）的访问控制策略，通过形式化建模和信息流图来识别设备间非预期的信息流动带来的安全漏洞，并实现工具IOT:POKER进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网云提供商（如AWS IoT Core）提供访问控制机制，但其配置的安全至关重要。在设备具有不同信任级别或分布在不同子系统的场景中，仅仅孤立地验证权限是不够的，需要分析设备间是否存在非预期的信息流安全漏洞。

Method: 作者对AWS IoT Core的组件进行形式化建模，定义信息流图来捕获访问控制策略所允许的设备间通信。利用SMT求解器构建图的有限表示，从而支持对设备间信息流进行验证。实现为一个名为IOT:POKER的工具。

Result: 在真实场景和多个真实世界策略上对IOT:POKER工具进行了评估。

Conclusion: 通过形式化建模和SMT求解器辅助的信息流图验证方法，能够有效识别物联网访问控制策略中设备间非预期信息流导致的安全漏洞。

Abstract: Many cloud providers for IoT technologies offer access control mechanisms whose proper configuration is critical for security. However, verifying permissions in isolation is insufficient in a setting where devices have different levels of trust or are compartmentalised in various subsystems. This work analyses IoT access control policies to identify potential security vulnerabilities from unwanted information flow between devices. To this end, we formally model AWS IoT Core's components and define an information flow graph to capture the communication among devices permitted by the access control policies. We build a finite representation of the graph by leveraging an SMT solver, thus enabling the verification of information flow between devices. We implement our approach in a tool called IOT:POKER, and assess it on a realistic scenario and several real-world policies.

</details>


### [86] [Agent Harness Distillation: Inference-Time Harness Extraction and Exploitation in Autonomous Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28147)
*Yu Cui,Wuli Yang,Yirui Shi,Junhao Xia,Hui Jiang,Lei Gao,Chenfu Bao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agent Harness Distillation (AHD)框架，首次系统研究自主多智能体系统（AMAS）中推理时涌现的harness（推理时编排逻辑）的IP泄露风险。AHD通过黑盒交互，分两个阶段（预蒸馏和后蒸馏）从目标智能体中提取harness行为模式，实验证明其有效性并揭示了严重的IP泄露问题，同时提出了一种基于欺骗的防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有工作研究了静态多智能体系统中的IP泄露，但AMAS中harness行为是在推理过程中动态涌现的，其安全性风险尚未被探讨。考虑到构建这些推理时harness需要大量的工程和计算资源，属于有价值的知识产权，因此需要研究和量化这一新的安全威胁。

Method: AHD框架包含两个阶段：1）预蒸馏阶段——通过黑盒交互从目标智能体的响应中推断其推理时harness行为并构建初始harness；2）后蒸馏阶段——迭代精炼初始harness以对齐目标智能体的行为模式。同时提出了基于欺骗的防御策略，在不损害受保护智能体可用性的前提下降低harness提取的有效性。

Result: 在多个真实AMAS和多种骨干LLM上的实验表明，AHD能够有效提取推理时harness能力，揭示了显著的IP泄露风险；同时验证了所提出的欺骗式防御方法能够在保持智能体效用性的同时有效降低harness提取的成功率。

Conclusion: 本文首次将harness提取形式化为AMAS中的安全问题进行系统研究，发现了一个此前未被充分探索的安全威胁，并为该问题提出了可行的防御方向。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent systems (AMAS) built on large language models (LLMs), such as Hermes, increasingly rely on inference-time harnesses to coordinate reasoning and action. Constructing these harnesses requires substantial engineering effort and computational resources, as they are iteratively optimized over a combinatorial search space while co-evolving with the underlying LLM. Inference-time harnesses therefore constitute valuable intellectual property (IP). Although prior work has investigated IP leakage in static multi-agent systems with pre-configured architectures, it remains unclear whether similar risks arise in AMAS, where harness behavior emerges dynamically during inference. To address this gap, we introduce Agent Harness Distillation (AHD), a framework for studying the security risks arising from inference-time harness extraction in AMAS. We formalize harness extraction as a new security problem and develop an evaluation framework for quantifying such risks. AHD extracts inference-time harness capabilities from a target agent through black-box interactions and consists of two stages. In the pre-distillation stage, AHD infers inference-time harness behaviors from the responses of the target agent and constructs an initial harness. In the post-distillation stage, AHD iteratively refines the initial harness to align with the behavioral patterns of the target agent. Experiments on real-world AMAS across multiple backbone LLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of AHD and reveal substantial IP leakage risks. We further propose a deception-based defense that reduces harness extraction effectiveness while preserving the utility of the protected agent. Our findings uncover a previously underexplored security threat to AMAS.

</details>


### [87] [Piggybacking on Perception: Stealthy Concurrent Audio Prompt Injections against Multimodal LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28165)
*Mingxiao Liu,Yitong Li,Haoren Zhao,Yaoxiang Bian,Jianan Ma,Jian Zhang,Jialuo Chen,Xinhao Deng,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了针对多模态智能体的并发音频提示注入攻击，提出了指令增广与场景隐藏两种新型攻击技术，构建了首个音频指令注入攻击基准AudioAgentSecurity，并设计了基于源分离与一致性分析的级联防御机制CADV，在11个主流智能体上平均攻击成功率可达69.10%，防御检测成功率超90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的多模态智能体通过连续音频交互执行自主任务已成为趋势，但音频输入中不可避免地包含环境噪声，这为攻击者提供了可利用的攻击面。传统的针对语音设备的声学攻击技术已不适用，而并发音频提示注入攻击这一关键威胁尚未被系统探索，缺乏统一的评估基准和有效防御手段。

Method: 1) 提出指令增广与场景隐藏两种新型攻击技术，使恶意音频指令可隐蔽地"搭载"在用户语音上，劫持智能体执行恶意行为；2) 构建AudioAgentSecurity基准，覆盖8个真实任务场景和10种不同攻击模式；3) 设计级联音频解耦与验证（CADV）防御机制，结合声学源分离和跨模态一致性分析来检测音频指令注入。

Result: 在包括Gemini 3 Pro和GPT-4o-audio在内的11个最先进智能体上进行评估，所提方法对Gemini 3 Pro的平均攻击成功率（ASR）达69.10%；CADV防御机制在多种攻击向量上实现超过90%的检测成功率；在豆包AI智能手机上的真人志愿者真实世界实验证实攻击具有高隐蔽性和有效性，同时防御措施能可靠缓解这些漏洞。

Conclusion: 并发音频提示注入是多模态智能体面临的重大安全威胁，现有基于提示层面的防御不足。CADV通过声学源分离和跨模态一致性分析提供了更稳健的防御方案，真实场景验证了攻防两端的有效性，为多模态智能体的音频安全提供了首个系统性基准和防御框架。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-driven multimodal agents are increasingly deployed to execute autonomous tasks via continuous audio interaction. While this paradigm enhances interaction naturalness, it introduces a critical yet under-explored attack surface, as audio inputs inevitably contain environmental noise beyond user control. In this paper, we investigate concurrent audio prompt injection attacks targeting multimodal agents. Distinct from traditional acoustic attacks on voice devices, we propose novel techniques for instruction augmentation and scenario concealment. These methods allow malicious audio instructions to imperceptibly "piggyback" onto user speech, thereby hijacking agents to execute malicious actions. To systematically quantify this threat, we construct AudioAgentSecurity, the first comprehensive benchmark for audio instruction injection attacks, encompassing 8 real-world task scenarios and 10 distinct attack patterns. We evaluate 11 state-of-the-art agents, including Gemini 3 Pro and GPT-4o-audio. Notably, our methods achieve an average Attack Success Rate (ASR) of 69.10\% against the advanced Gemini 3 Pro. To counter this threat, we further introduce Cascaded Audio Decoupling and Verification (CADV), a defense mechanism based on source separation and consistency analysis. Compared with existing prompt-level defenses, CADV leverages acoustic source separation and cross-modal consistency analysis to detect audio instruction injections more robustly, achieving over 90\% detection success across diverse attack vectors. Finally, real-world experiments with human volunteers on Doubao AI Smartphone in diverse dynamic real-world scenarios confirm the attacks' high stealth and efficacy, while demonstrating that our defense reliably mitigates these vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [88] [Secure Aggregation for Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning on Clinical EEG Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28191)
*Pouya Rajabi,Mohsen Toorani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于掩码安全聚合的隐私保护联邦学习框架，用于临床脑电图（EEG）数据，结合图通信、阈值秘密共享、抗丢失聚合、裁剪、布隆过滤器记录链接初始化及辅助公证人可验证性，支持半诚实和恶意两种聚合场景，并在TUH EEG模拟跨机构医疗环境中验证了其安全性与训练兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许多个机构共享模型训练而无需交换原始临床EEG数据，但单个模型的更新仍可能泄露隐私，现有方法无法完全防止这种隐私泄漏。

Method: 以掩码式安全聚合为核心保护机制，框架包含基于图的通信、阈值秘密共享、抗掉线聚合、本地更新裁剪、可选的基于布隆过滤器的隐私保护记录链接初始化模块，以及辅助公证人可验证性；支持半诚实和恶意两种聚合设置，基于Flower联邦学习框架实现。

Result: 在模拟跨机构医疗环境（TUH EEG数据）中评估了安全聚合变体在不同客户端配置下的表现。结果表明安全变体能够向聚合服务器隐藏个体更新，且与联邦模型训练兼容；恶意设置下的防护措施和轻量级一致性检查机制引入了额外计算、通信和轮次耗时开销。半诚实变体开销最低，恶意和辅助公证人变体提供更强的一致性、完整性和轻量级验证支持但成本更高。

Conclusion: 在给定假设下，所提出的安全联邦学习变体能有效保护个体更新不被聚合服务器窥探，同时保持与联邦模型训练的兼容性；半诚实变体在安全性-开销权衡中表现最优，恶意和公证人变体则以更高成本换取更强的安全保证。

Abstract: Federated learning enables multiple institutions to train shared models without exchanging raw clinical EEG data, but it does not fully prevent privacy leakage from individual model updates. This paper presents a privacy-preserving federated learning framework for clinical EEG data using masking-based secure aggregation as the core protection mechanism. The framework combines graph-based communication, threshold secret sharing, dropout-resilient aggregation, local update clipping, an optional Bloom filter-based privacy-preserving record-linkage initialization module, and auxiliary-notary-based verifiability. It supports both semi-honest and malicious aggregation settings and is implemented using the Flower federated learning framework. The secure-aggregation variants are evaluated in a simulated cross-silo healthcare setting using TUH EEG-derived data under different client configurations. Under the stated assumptions, the secure variants hide individual updates from the aggregation server. The results show that these variants remain compatible with federated model training, although malicious-setting safeguards and lightweight consistency-checking mechanisms introduce additional computation, communication, and round-duration overhead. The semi-honest variant provides the lowest overhead among the secure configurations, while malicious and auxiliary-notary variants offer stronger consistency, integrity, and lightweight verification support at higher cost.

</details>


### [89] [Security of World-Model-Based Embodied AI: A Lifecycle of Threats, Defenses, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28226)
*Fazhong Liu,Zhuoyan Chen,Haozhen Tan,Yan Meng,Guoxing Chen,Haojin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了具身AI世界模型的安全威胁，追踪其从数据构建、表征学习、状态接地、想象推理到轨迹执行与长期适应的全生命周期风险，并提出生命周期分类体系，同时揭示世界模型作为运行时安全屏障与其被攻破后产生"预测性安全幻觉"的双重性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型为具身AI提供了预测核心（压缩观测为状态、模拟行动条件未来、支持超越反应式控制的规划），但这引入了新的安全边界——数据、传感器、提示或反馈中的攻击可能渗透到物理动作层面。缺乏对世界模型全生命周期安全威胁的系统性分析。

Method: 采用全生命周期视角进行威胁追踪，涵盖数据构建、表征学习、状态接地、想象、轨迹评估、执行及通过记忆和工具的长期适应各阶段。将熟悉的攻击家族（投毒、后门、对抗样本、传感器欺骗、提示注入、轨迹操纵、供应链攻击）映射到世界模型各环节，提出生命周期分类体系、安全属性映射、评估协议及防御框架（涵盖来源追溯、稳健接地、不确定性感知预测、轨迹门控、反馈审计和部署保障）。

Result: 揭示同一攻击家族在破坏世界状态、学习动力学、可利用性估计或安全成本时具有不同含义；指出世界模型具有双重角色——既可作为运行时安全屏障，又会在被攻破或过度信任时产生预测性安全幻觉；提供了生命周期分类法并系统映射了现有攻击到世界模型安全属性，并给出安全失败评估协议。

Conclusion: 世界模型的安全问题必须在全生命周期中系统性审视，而非作为孤立组件处理；防御需要涵盖来源追溯、稳健接地、不确定性感知预测、轨迹门控、反馈审计及部署保障等多层结构，并警惕预测性安全幻觉这一特有风险。

Abstract: World models give embodied AI a predictive core: they compress observations into states, simulate action-conditioned futures, and enable planning beyond reactive control. This predictive layer, however, opens a new security boundary-compromise can propagate from data, sensors, prompts, or feedback into physical action. Rather than treating world models as an isolated component, this survey traces threats across their entire lifecycle-from data construction and representation learning, through state grounding and imagination, to trajectory evaluation, execution, and long-term adaptation via memory and tools. We show that familiar attack families: poisoning, backdoors, adversarial examples, sensor spoofing, prompt injection, trajectory manipulation, and supply-chain attacks take on distinct meanings when they corrupt world states, learned dynamics, affordance estimates, or safety costs. We also highlight a duality: world models can serve as runtime safety shields, yet when compromised or over-trusted they generate predictive safety illusions. The survey offers a lifecycle taxonomy, maps existing attacks to world-model security properties, outlines evaluation protocols for safety failures, and structures defenses across provenance, robust grounding, uncertainty-aware prediction, trajectory gating, feedback auditing, and deployment assurance.

</details>


### [90] [Implementing Homomorphic Encryption-Based Logic Locking in System-on-Chip Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28542)
*Ye Ziyang,Makoto Ikeda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二元环学习误差（Ring-LWE）算法的逻辑锁定方案，实现在RISC-V系统级芯片设计中。该方法在特权切换过程中保护关键逻辑路径，无需向用户暴露敏感锁定参数。锁定模块消耗3519个LUT和2645个寄存器，相比基线系统产生6.0%的LUT和6.9%的寄存器开销，解锁过程约需2.6微秒，主要影响系统级操作而保持用户级计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逻辑锁定方法需要向用户提供原始锁定参数，存在安全隐患。作者旨在设计一种无需暴露敏感参数的逻辑锁定方案，以保护RISC-V SoC中特权切换过程中的关键逻辑路径，在提升安全性的同时控制硬件开销和性能影响。

Method: 采用基于二进制Ring-LWE算法的逻辑锁定机制，将其集成到RISC-V SoC设计中。该方案在特权切换路径中植入锁定模块，通过数学难题保证安全性，使用户无法逆向获取敏感锁定参数，同时仅对系统级操作产生适度性能影响。

Result: 锁定模块占用3519个LUT和2645个寄存器，导致整体硬件开销为LUT增加6.0%、寄存器增加6.9%。解锁过程耗时约2.6微秒，性能影响集中在系统级操作，用户级计算效率得以保持。

Conclusion: 该方案在安全性与开销之间取得了合理平衡，证明了基于Ring-LWE的硬件逻辑锁定在RISC-V SoC中的可行性与有效性，尤其适用于需要保护系统级安全关键操作的应用场景。

Abstract: This study presents a logic locking scheme based on the binary Ring Learning With Errors algorithm, implemented in a RISC-V System-on-Chip design. Unlike traditional logic locking methods that require providing users with raw locking parameters, the proposed approach secures critical logic paths in the privilege switching process without exposing these sensitive parameters. The implemented locking module itself consumes 3519 Look-Up Tables and 2645 Registers, leading to an overall overhead of 6.0% in Look-Up Tables and 6.9% in Registers compared to the baseline system. The unlock process requires about 2.6 us, introducing moderate performance impact, primarily affecting system-level operations while preserving user-level computational efficiency.

</details>


### [91] [Formalization of security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28551)
*Gilles Barthe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本章探讨了证明助手在系统安全、基于语言的安全、安全编译和密码学等领域的应用，其动机包括验证设计与实现满足预期安全属性，以及支持认证流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明助手常被用于验证设计与实现是否满足预期的安全属性，同时进一步支持安全相关的认证过程。

Method: 本章从四个方向展开论述：系统安全、基于语言的安全、安全编译和密码学，展示证明助手在这些领域的具体应用。

Result: 系统性地阐述了证明助手在多个安全相关领域中的应用场景与实践。

Conclusion: 证明助手不仅能用于安全属性的形式化验证，还能为安全认证提供有力支撑，在系统安全、语言安全、安全编译和密码学等方向均具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Proof assistants are often used to validate that designs and implementations meet their expected security properties. A further motivation for using proof assistants is to support certification. This chapter focuses on their applications to system security, language-based security, secure compilation, and cryptography.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [92] [Multi-Head Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27230)
*Cheng Luo,Zefan Cai,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多头注意力残差（MHAR），将注意力残差中的单一查询改写为H个逐子空间头，每个头拥有独立的softmax，从而允许不同特征子空间对深度历史采用不同的读取分布。MHAR在100M、350M和1B规模上均优于标准Transformer及三种基线方法，验证损失分别降低-0.061、-0.149和-0.140，H=4或8为最优头数，并通过Triton核优化使训练吞吐量接近基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer通过单一加法残差流跨深度传播信息，注意力残差虽通过softmax让每层读取深度历史，但使用单一共享查询，迫使所有特征子空间共用同一读取分布。当模型宽度增大时，不同子空间对各层读取的偏好分歧增大，这种强制妥协的成本也随之上升，因此需要一种能按子空间独立路由深度历史的机制。

Method: 将注意力残差的路由查询重塑为H个逐子空间头，每个头拥有独立的softmax，使读取变为块对角结构。该重塑操作不增加参数且计算开销可忽略，H=1时精确退化为注意力残差。作者在去重、质量过滤且以STEM和代码为主的Nemotron退火语料上从头训练模型，并使用融合Triton路由核加速计算，同时引入delta注意力残差实现等效保真转换以支持8B中途训练。

Result: 在100M、350M和1B规模下验证损失分别降低-0.061、-0.149和-0.140，在所有设置中四个方法里表现最佳，且增益随规模增大而增大。头数H是真实的设计轴而非自由旋钮：验证损失对H呈U型曲线，在H=4或8处平坦最优，H=16过度分割会回吐部分收益。训练查询直接探测确认子空间分歧是驱动因素。Triton路由核将训练吞吐量从基线0.2-0.5x提升至0.55-0.88x，峰值内存接近基线。8B中途训练在GSM8K上+3.2、GPQA上+3.1。

Conclusion: MHAR通过为每个特征子空间提供独立的深度路由分布，有效缓解了宽模型中子空间读取偏好分歧带来的信息传播瓶颈。头数H是需要仔细调优的结构超参数，子空间分歧是增益的机制性根源。MHAR在性能、训练效率和可扩展性方面均展现出优势，适合应用于大规模语言模型训练。

Abstract: Transformers propagate information across depth through a single additive residual stream: every sublayer reads only the most recent state. Attention residuals relax this by letting each sublayer attend, through a learned softmax. However, that read uses a single query shared across the entire width, so every feature subspace must read the depth history through one distribution. The cost of this forced compromise grows with how much the subspaces disagree about which layers to read, and disagreement grows with model width. We introduce Multi-Head Attention Residuals (MHAR): the routing query is reshaped into H per-subspace heads, each with its own softmax over the depth history. The read becomes block-diagonal, the reshape adds zero parameters and negligible compute, and H = 1 recovers attention residuals exactly. Trained from scratch on a deduplicated Nemotron-based anneal corpus that is quality-filtered and STEM- and code-heavy, MHAR improves validation loss over a standard Transformer at 100M, 350M, and 1B (-0.061, -0.149, and -0.140). It achieves the best result among four methods in every setting, with the gain increasing from 100M to the larger scales. The head count is a real design axis rather than a free knob: validation loss is U-shaped with respect to H, with a flat optimum at H = 4 or H = 8 across scales. We adopt H = 8 for large-scale models; over-splitting beyond this point (H = 16) consistently gives back part of the gain. A direct probe of the trained queries confirms that learned subspace disagreement is the underlying driver. Fused Triton routing kernels increase attention-residual training throughput from 0.2-0.5x to 0.55-0.88x of the baseline while maintaining near-baseline peak memory. An identity-preserving conversion using delta attention residuals supports 8B mid-training, yielding improvements of +3.2 on GSM8K and +3.1 on GPQA.

</details>


### [93] [KernelGenBench: A Multi-Source and Multi-Chip Benchmark for LLM-based Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27231)
*Peiyu Zang,Jian Tao,Jialing Zhang,Yichen Yuan,Wentao Zhang,Guang Liu,Yonghua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KernelGenBench是一个统一基准，用于系统评估LLM和智能体生成的Triton内核，涵盖多算子来源（210个算子）和多硬件平台（6种异构平台），大规模评估（超过150亿token）显示智能体方法优于纯LLM采样方法，但跨平台性能退化严重且生成成本极高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM大幅增加了对高效加速器内核的需求，但内核开发高度专业且劳动密集。虽然LLM和智能体框架为自动内核生成提供了新途径，但缺乏一个全面的基准来严格评估LLM生成的内核在不同算子来源和异构硬件平台上的表现。

Method: 提出KernelGenBench统一基准，包含两个子基准：(1) KernelGenBench-MS（多来源），评估来自三个来源的210个算子；(2) KernelGenBench-MC（多芯片），使用110个算子子集测量六种异构硬件平台上的性能可移植性。大规模评估消耗超过150亿token。

Result: (1) 智能体方法一致优于纯LLM采样方法，cuBLAS算子最具挑战性；(2) 生成性能在不同硬件平台上差异显著，专精内核的智能体遭遇严重跨平台性能退化（AutoKernel从NVIDIA上87%降至平台E上25%）；(3) 自主内核生成成本极高，专精智能体方法平均每个成功算子消耗511万token（AKO4all达519万token），比简单LLM采样方法高出数个数量级。

Conclusion: 当前基于LLM和智能体的自动内核生成虽然在算子来源多样性和硬件平台覆盖方面已有明显进展，但仍面临跨平台可移植性差和计算成本高昂两大核心挑战，需要进一步完善。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have significantly increased the demand for efficient accelerator kernels, but kernel development remains a highly specialized and labor-intensive task. The recent rise of LLMs and agentic frameworks offers a promising pathway toward automatic kernel generation. However, despite rapid progress, there is still no comprehensive benchmark to rigorously evaluate LLM-generated kernels across diverse operator sources or heterogeneous hardware platforms. We present KernelGenBench, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating LLM- and agent-generated Triton kernels across diverse operator sources and heterogeneous hardware platforms. It comprises two complementary sub-benchmarks: KernelGenBench-MS (Multi-Source), evaluating 210 operators from three sources beyond standard PyTorch-centric tasks, and KernelGenBench-MC (Multi-Chip), measuring performance portability across six heterogeneous hardware platforms using a 110-operator subset. Our large-scale evaluation, consuming over 15 billion tokens, shows: (1) agent-based methods consistently outperform pure LLM sampling methods, while cuBLAS operators are the most challenging across all methods; (2) generation performance varies significantly across hardware platforms, with even recent kernel-specialized agents experiencing severe cross-platform degradation (e.g., AutoKernel drops from 87% on NVIDIA to 25% on Platform E); (3) autonomous kernel generation remains highly cost-intensive, with specialized agent methods averaging 5.11 million tokens per successful operator (AKO4all reaches 5.19 million), orders of magnitude higher than simple LLM sampling approaches.

</details>


### [94] [RadHarmony: Radiological Data Handling in the Era of Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27235)
*Frank Li,Bardia Khosravi,Mohammadreza Chavoshi,Theo Dapamede,YoungSeok Jeon,Janice Newsome,Hari Trivedi,Judy Gichoya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RadHarmony是一个开源的Python库，提供统一API用于加载、协调和增强放射学数据集，重点支持胸部X光片，兼顾CT和MRI。它将24个公开数据集的标准元数据统一为表格格式，基于MONAI提供深度学习就绪的样本交付、磁盘缓存、多模态标注支持（分类标签、分割掩膜、边界框和放射学报告文本），并引入AI-agent技能辅助新数据集集成，通过跨三个异构胸部X光数据集的预训练实验验证了其无需数据集特定代码的可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在放射学图像上训练深度学习模型需要整合来自不同来源的异构数据集，这些数据集在文件格式、目录布局、标签模式和标注类型上存在差异，导致数据集成成本高、重复工作量大。

Method: 构建一个统一的Python库RadHarmony：(1) 将24个公开数据集的元数据标准化为单一表格格式；(2) 封装MONAI的map-style数据集，提供深度学习就绪的样本交付和可选的磁盘缓存；(3) 通过单一接口支持分类标签、分割掩膜、边界框和放射学报告文本；(4) 提供交互式可视化工具用于数据集探索和验证；(5) 引入AI-agent技能，引导从原始数据检查到代码生成和测试的完整集成流程。

Result: 通过预训练RadHarmony-ViT参考视觉Transformer基线模型进行验证，该模型能够组合三个异构胸部X光数据集且无需数据集特定代码。代码和预训练模型权重已开源。

Conclusion: RadHarmony有效降低了放射学异构数据集集成的壁垒，使研究人员能够以统一的接口高效利用多个公开数据集进行深度学习模型训练，并通过AI-agent技能进一步自动化了新数据集的集成过程。

Abstract: Training deep learning models on radiological images requires integrating heterogeneous datasets across different sources, file formats, directory layouts, label schemas, and annotation types. We present RadHarmony, an open-source Python library that provides a unified API for loading, harmonizing, and augmenting radiological datasets, with a primary focus on chest radiographs and early support for computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). RadHarmony standardizes metadata from 24 public datasets into a single tabular format, wraps MONAI's map-style datasets for deep-learning-ready sample delivery with optional on-disk caching, and supports classification labels, segmentation masks, bounding boxes, and radiology report text through a single interface, with an interactive visualization tool for dataset exploration and verification. To lower the barrier for integrating new datasets, RadHarmony introduces an AI-agent skill that guides the full integration workflow from raw data inspection through code generation and testing. We demonstrate the library's utility by pretraining RadHarmony-ViT, a reference vision transformer baseline that combines three heterogeneous chest radiograph datasets with no dataset-specific code. The code and pretrained model weights are available at https://github.com/f10409/RadHarmony.

</details>


### [95] [Asymmetric Collapse in Model Merging: When Refusal Over- writes Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27240)
*Aarnav Choudhary,Matheus Fonseca Rocha,Jiwon Seo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过Gemma-3-1B-IT在CARES危害分级和WildJailbreak对抗性拒答两个安全问题上的微调模型，研究了四种标准模型合并方法（Linear、SLERP、TIES、DARE-TIES）对安全相关行为的保持效果。发现攻击拒答能力显著迁移（81-85%保持率），而分类准确率几乎崩溃（最高12.9%），尽管两个任务向量近正交（余弦相似度0.011），但拒答微调产生更大的逐层任务向量幅度，导致幅度敏感方法偏向拒答更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并常用于在不进行额外训练的情况下组合多个微调模型的能力，但尚不清楚标准合并方法能否同时保持多种安全相关行为。研究者希望探究这一点，以理解合并方法在安全场景中的局限性。

Method: 采用受控案例研究：使用两个Gemma-3-1B-IT微调模型，分别针对CARES危害等级分类和WildJailbreak对抗性拒答两个互补的安全目标。使用Linear、SLERP、TIES和DARE-TIES四种合并方法，在分类准确率、攻击抵抗力和良性合规性三个维度上评估合并模型，并进行权重空间分析测量任务向量方向和大小的关系。

Result: 四种合并方法下攻击拒答率保持81-85%，而CARES分类准确率降至最多12.9%。两个任务向量几乎正交（余弦相似度0.011），但拒答微调在每层产生更大的任务向量幅度，导致幅度敏感的合并方法倾向于保留拒答更新而丧失分类能力。

Conclusion: 标准模型合并方法在安全相关任务向量尺度差异显著时，会将安全识别能力退化为广泛拒答行为。任务向量的幅度差异（而非方向冲突）是导致安全能力不对称迁移的关键因素，提示合并时需考虑任务向量的尺度匹配。

Abstract: Model merging is often used to combine capabilities from separately fine-tuned models without additional training, but it is unclear whether standard merging methods preserve multiple safety-relevant behaviors simultaneously. We study this question through a controlled case study using two Gemma-3-1B-IT finetunes on two complementary safety objectives: CARES harm-level classification and WildJailbreak adversarial refusal. We merge the two fine-tunes using Linear, SLERP, TIES, and DARE-TIES, and evaluate the merged models on classification accuracy, attack resistance, and benign compliance. Across all four methods, attack resistance transfers significantly more than classification accuracy: merged models retain 81-85% jailbreak refusal rates while CARES accuracy falls to at most 12.9%. Weight-space measurements suggest that this asymmetry is not caused by strongly opposing task-vector directions: the two task vectors are nearly orthogonal (cosine similarity 0.011). Instead, the refusal fine-tune induces consistently larger per-layer task-vector magnitudes, causing magnitude-sensitive methods to favor refusal updates. These results show that standard model merging can collapse safety recognition into broad refusal when safety-relevant task vectors differ substantially in scale.

</details>


### [96] [Divergence Decoding: Training-Free Capability Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27248)
*Yimi Wang,Hao Li,Shuo Yang,He Cao,Dechen Zhang,Ziang Wu,Zhiyuan Yan,Fanyang Mo,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Divergence Decoding，一种无需训练的能力融合框架，通过Jensen-Shannon散度监控通用模型与领域专家模型在每个token上的分布分歧，在专家出现潜在推理风险时动态切换到通用模型，实现推理时的自适应能力组合，在GPQA、ChemBench等科学基准上超越单一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型存在"通用性与专业性"的两难困境：通用模型（generalists）推理能力强但缺乏特定科学领域的专业知识；领域模型（specialists）知识渊博但存在推理能力下降和鲁棒性减弱等专业化副作用。现有方法无法同时兼顾两者的优势。

Method: Divergence Decoding是一种训练免费的推理框架，借鉴了投机解码（speculative decoding）的"草稿-验证"（draft-and-verify）结构，将其重构为自适应路由机制。核心思想是使用Jensen-Shannon散度在每个token上监测通用模型与专家模型之间的分布分歧：当专家模型表现出显著分歧时，判定其可能存在推理风险，立即将控制权路由给通用模型，从而在不损害领域专业知识的前提下动态注入通用推理能力。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama系列模型上，针对GPQA、ChemBench和ChemCoTBench等具有挑战性的科学基准进行评估，实验结果显示Divergence Decoding同时优于领域专家模型和通用模型，且超过了大多数单一模型基线的性能。

Conclusion: Divergence Decoding提供了一种通用的、无需训练的范式，通过自适应推理时协作来融合不同LLM的能力，有效解决了通用模型与专家模型之间的能力权衡问题。

Abstract: While large language models excel in reasoning, these generalists often lack knowledge for specialized scientific domains. Conversely, domain models~(specialists), while knowledgeable, suffer from specialization side-effects including diminished logic and reduced robustness.To address this dilemma, we introduce Divergence Decoding, a training-free framework for capability fusion. It reconstructs the "draft-and-verify" skeleton of speculative decoding into an adaptive routing mechanism. The core is using Jensen-Shannon divergence to monitor the distributional disagreement between the two models at each token. When the specialist exhibits significant divergence, our method identifies it as a potential reasoning risk and instantaneously routes control to the generalist. This allows the dynamic injection of general reasoning while preserving domain expertise, achieving inference-time policy composition of the generalist and the specialist.We evaluate Divergence Decoding across diverse model families (Qwen and Llama series) on challenging scientific benchmarks (GPQA, ChemBench, and ChemCoTBench). Experimental results demonstrate that Divergence Decoding outperforms both the domain-specialized and general-purpose models, effectively surpassing the performance of most single-model baseline. This suggests that Divergence Decoding provides a general, training-free paradigm for fusing diverse LLM capabilities through adaptive inference-time collaboration.

</details>


### [97] [PIE-APT: A Unified Framework for Temporal Planning and Contradiction Hunting via Incremental Direct-Derivation Abduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27287)
*Amir Hossein Sharafi,Alireza Shahbazi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的推理与规划框架（PIE-Abducer + PIE-APT），在表达力丰富的描述逻辑（DL）上原生进行动态知识图谱（DKG）中的时间轴状态推理与规划，通过直接导出式溯因规避最小碰集枚举，并采用递归Generate-and-Test架构实现有界因果深度的溯因规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态知识图谱中的推理与规划面临开放世界不完整信息的挑战：现有动作形式主义存在可判定性问题与Ramification Problem，而通过结构溯因管理不完整知识需要大规模组合搜索，效率低下。

Method: 提出两个集成模块：（1）PIE-Abducer，采用增量直接导出式溯因，通过将目标否定注入一致分支并提取反证后果来发现缺失前提，规避传统最小碰集枚举；（2）PIE-APT，采用递归Generate-and-Test架构，将反向链式A*搜索与PIE-Abducer交织至有界因果深度，再通过前向链式时间投影进行严格验证。状态转换沿线性时间轴建模为对演绎封闭DL理论的非单调更新，动作在OWL中原生表示。

Result: 在四个OWL基准上评估，覆盖经典规划中不存在的语义能力（参数化目标见证搜索、中途DL蕴涵、开放世界假设注入、对抗性矛盾发现），结果证明该方法在质量上优于经典规划器，且直接导出式溯因在溯因扩充阶段数量上优于最小碰集忠实的基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于描述逻辑的原生表示与直接导出式溯因相结合，能够在保持逻辑可判定性的同时有效解决动态知识图谱上的开放世界不完整信息推理与规划问题，优于经典规划方法。

Abstract: Reasoning and planning over Dynamic Knowledge Graphs (DKGs) present significant challenges, especially in open-world environments with incomplete information. Existing action formalisms often face decidability issues and the Ramification Problem, while managing incomplete knowledge via structural abduction requires expansive combinatorial search. This paper introduces a unified framework with two integrated modules---\textbf{PIE-Abducer} (incremental direct-derivation abduction) and \textbf{PIE-APT} (Abductive Planning for Temporal KGs)---operating natively on the highly expressive Description Logic. We model state transitions along a linear timeline as non-monotonic updates to deductively closed DL theories. Treating the incremental reasoner as a black-box and representing actions natively in OWL without external modal operators preserves logical decidability. To address incomplete knowledge, \textbf{PIE-Abducer} circumvents traditional Minimal Hitting Set (MHS) enumeration. Instead of combinatorial syntactic search, it injects the logical negation of a target goal into a consistent branch and extracts missing premises via direct refutation consequences. \textbf{PIE-APT} then employs a recursive \textit{Generate-and-Test} architecture, interleaving backward-chaining A* search with \textbf{PIE-Abducer} up to a bounded causal depth, followed by strict validation via forward-chaining Temporal Projection. We evaluate four OWL benchmarks stressing semantic abilities absent in classical planning: parameterized goals with witness search, mid-search DL entailment, open-world assumption injection, and adversarial contradiction hunting. Results demonstrate qualitative superiority over classical planners and prove our direct-derivation approach quantitatively outperforms an MHS-faithful baseline during abductive enrichment.

</details>


### [98] [PAUSE: A User-Centric Benchmark for Personal AI Assistants in Unified Service Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27354)
*Haoyu Chen,Xirui Shi,Yuyao Wang,Jerry Chen,Di Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PAUSE，一个用于评估个人AI助手在有状态、服务集成环境中表现的用户中心基准测试，涵盖了多轮交互中的状态推理、配置感知和授权约束等核心挑战。实验表明最先进的专有模型在需要状态推理和配置感知的任务上完成率不足70%，并呈现出一致的可解释失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试存在两个关键局限：一是将服务上下文碎片化，二是抽象掉了用户状态，因此无法在真实服务场景中评估以用户为中心的个人助手行为。实际部署中，AI助手需要处理持久化用户状态、尊重用户特定配置和权限，并在多服务间维持长时程、带约束的交互，这些能力在现有基准中难以被有效评估。

Method: PAUSE基准通过以下方式实现：(1)构建有状态的、服务集成环境，要求代理跨异构用户自有资源协调动作；(2)引入现实用户模拟实现显式用户-代理交互，超越静态工具执行；(3)采用多机制评估框架：开放式的服务管理任务使用语义和轨迹级行为指标，约束密集型任务采用确定性、基于状态的验证；(4)提出用户中心合成管道，可扩展生成连贯的服务环境、用户配置和可靠标注的任务。

Result: 基准测试结果显示，即使是最先进的专有模型，在需要状态推理和配置感知的场景中任务完成率也未能达到70%，并且暴露出一致且可解释的失败模式。这表明当前模型在用户中心个人助手场景中的状态推理和配置感知能力仍存在显著不足。

Conclusion: PAUSE为个人AI助手的用户中心评估提供了更真实、可复现的基准框架。其用户中心合成管道支持基准的可扩展性和未来研究，为后续改进个人AI助手在有状态、多服务环境中的部署能力指明了方向。

Abstract: Personal AI assistants are increasingly deployed as task-oriented, tool-augmented agents that operate within unified service environments to support everyday user activities. In realistic settings, such assistants must reason over persistent user state, respect user-specific configurations and permissions, and sustain long-horizon, constraint-aware interactions across multiple services. Existing benchmarks, however, often fragment service contexts or abstract away user state, limiting their ability to evaluate user-centric personal assistant behavior in realistic service settings. We introduce PAUSE, a user-centric benchmark for evaluating personal AI assistants in stateful, service-integrated environments. PAUSE captures core challenges of real-world assistant deployment by requiring agents to coordinate actions across heterogeneous user-owned resources while maintaining consistency with environment state, authorization constraints over multi-turn interactions. The benchmark incorporates explicit user-agent interaction via realistic user simulation, enabling evaluation beyond static tool execution. To support principled and reproducible evaluation, PAUSE adopts a multi-regime evaluation framework aligned with task characteristics. Open-ended service management tasks are assessed using semantic and trajectory-level behavioral metrics, while constraint-intensive tasks admit deterministic, state-based verification. Benchmark results show that even state-of-the-art proprietary models fail to reach 70% task completion on scenarios requiring stateful reasoning and configuration awareness, revealing consistent and interpretable failure patterns. Finally, we present a user-centric synthesis pipeline that enables scalable generation of coherent service environments, user configurations, and reliably annotated tasks, supporting benchmark extensibility and future research.

</details>


### [99] [SkillMentor: LLM Agent Self-Evolution via Learning Blind-Spot Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27360)
*Xiaoyi Bao,Yuanzhen Xie,Yunzhi Tan,Jinghang Gu,Zhongqing Wang,Chu-Ren Huang,Bo Hu,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillMentor框架，通过强化学习训练一个独立的Mentor策略，使智能体能够自主发现自身盲点（即不知道的内容），将其转化为可复用的纠错技能，从而在不更新执行器权重、不依赖人工标注的情况下实现自我进化。在AppWorld和BFCLv3基准上平均提升执行器性能44.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体自我进化研究主要关注"如何行动"（执行层面），而忽略了"发现自己不知道什么"这一同样重要的能力。传统方法通常假设失败发现是给定的，只关注识别失败后的修复。作者提出盲点诊断本身是否可以被学习，并将其作为独立于执行的能力来研究。

Method: 提出SkillMentor框架：通过强化学习训练一个Mentor策略，使其具备三种能力：(1) 生成诊断任务来主动探测智能体的盲点；(2) 识别反复出现的失败模式；(3) 将这些失败模式整理为可复用的纠错技能。实验中排除了执行器适配和人工监督两个替代进步来源，确保所有改进都源自学习到的诊断能力。

Result: 在AppWorld和BFCLv3两个基准测试上，SkillMentor使执行器性能平均提升44.2%。这一提升不依赖于更新执行器权重或人工标注数据。

Conclusion: 盲点诊断是一种可学习的能力。通过将诊断与执行分离，智能体可以在不更新执行器参数、不依赖人工数据的情况下实现自我进化，这为智能体自我改进开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: Agent self-evolution has primarily focused on learning how to act, while overlooking an equally important capability: learning to discover what an agent does not know. Existing approaches typically assume that failure discovery is given, focusing on how to repair failures once they are identified. We ask whether blind-spot diagnosis itself can be learned. We thus study diagnosis as an agent capability separate from execution, and exclude two alternative sources of progress: executor adaptation and human supervision. Under these constraints, performance cannot improve through executor updates or annotated examples, forcing all improvements to originate from the learned diagnostic capability. We propose SkillMentor, which trains a Mentor policy via reinforcement learning to generate diagnostic tasks, identify recurrent failure modes, and curate them into reusable corrective skills. Across AppWorld and BFCLv3, SkillMentor improves executor performance by an average of 44.2%. These results suggest that blind-spot diagnosis is a learnable capability, enabling self-evolution without updating executor weights or relying on human-curated data.

</details>
